-- Drop privious database
DROP DATABASE IF EXISTS Faculty_Of_Technology;

-- Create database and use it
CREATE DATABASE IF NOT EXISTS Faculty_Of_Technology;

USE Faculty_Of_Technology;


-- Create user table and subtables of it
CREATE TABLE user (
    User_id INT AUTO_INCREMENT PRIMARY KEY,
    F_name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    L_name VARCHAR(50) NOT NULL,
    date_of_birth DATE,
    Street VARCHAR(100),
    City VARCHAR(100),
    Postal_code CHAR(50),
    Email VARCHAR(100) UNIQUE,
    Role VARCHAR(50),
    Password VARCHAR(100)
);


CREATE TABLE department (
    Department_id  CHAR(5) PRIMARY KEY,
    Dep_name VARCHAR(50));

-- create contac numbers table
CREATE TABLE user_contact_number (
	User_id INT,
	Phone_no VARCHAR(50),
	FOREIGN KEY(User_id) REFERENCES user(User_id)
);
    

CREATE TABLE admin(
    Admin_id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    FOREIGN KEY(Admin_id) REFERENCES user(User_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE dean (
    Dean_id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    FOREIGN KEY(Dean_id) REFERENCES user(User_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE student (
    Reg_no CHAR(20) PRIMARY KEY,
    User_id INT,
    Batch VARCHAR(20),
    Student_type VARCHAR(50),
    Dep_id  CHAR(5),
    FOREIGN KEY (Dep_id) REFERENCES department(Department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (User_id) REFERENCES USER(User_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE technical_officer (
    To_id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    FOREIGN KEY (To_id) REFERENCES USER(User_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

CREATE TABLE lecturer (
    Lecturer_id INT PRIMARY KEY,
    Dep_id  CHAR(5),
    FOREIGN KEY (Dep_id) REFERENCES department(Department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (Lecturer_id) REFERENCES USER(User_id) ON DELETE CASCADE
);


CREATE TABLE course (
    Course_code VARCHAR(20) PRIMARY KEY,
    Course_name VARCHAR(100),
    Type VARCHAR(50),
    Credits INT,
    Lecturer_in_charge INT,
    Dep_id  CHAR(5),
    FOREIGN KEY (Dep_id) REFERENCES department(Department_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (Lecturer_in_charge) REFERENCES lecturer(Lecturer_id)
);



CREATE TABLE MARK (
    Mark_id INT AUTO_INCREMENT  PRIMARY KEY,
    Reg_no CHAR(20),
    Course_code VARCHAR(20),
    Marks_type VARCHAR(50)CHECK ( Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3','Assignment_1','Assignment_2','Mini_project','Mid_practical','Mid_theory','End_practical','End_theory')),
    Marks_value DECIMAL(5,2) CHECK (Marks_value BETWEEN 0 AND 100),
    FOREIGN KEY (Reg_no) REFERENCES STUDENT(Reg_no),
    FOREIGN KEY (Course_code) REFERENCES COURSE(Course_code)
);

CREATE TABLE medical_record (
    Medical_id INT AUTO_INCREMENT  PRIMARY KEY,
    Reg_no CHAR(20),
    Session_date DATE,
    Reason VARCHAR(255),
    Approved BOOLEAN,
    FOREIGN KEY (Reg_no) REFERENCES STUDENT(Reg_no)
);

CREATE TABLE attendance (
    Attendance_id INT PRIMARY KEY AUTO_INCREMENT,
    Reg_no CHAR(20),
    Course_code VARCHAR(20),
    Session_date DATE,
    Session_type VARCHAR(10) CHECK (session_type IN ('Theory', 'Practical')),
    Session_hours DECIMAL(2,1) NOT NULL,
    Status VARCHAR(10) CHECK (status IN ('Present', 'Absent', 'Medical')),
    Medical_id INT,
    FOREIGN KEY (Medical_id) REFERENCES medical_record(Medical_id),
    FOREIGN KEY (reg_no) REFERENCES STUDENT(reg_no),
    FOREIGN KEY (course_code) REFERENCES COURSE(course_code)
);




-- create table for enrolls_in relationship between student and course
CREATE TABLE enrollment (
    Reg_no CHAR(20),
    Course_code VARCHAR(20),
    PRIMARY KEY (Reg_no,Course_code),
    FOREIGN KEY(Reg_no) REFERENCES student(Reg_no) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    FOREIGN KEY(Course_code) REFERENCES course(Course_code) ON DELETE CASCADE
);

UPDATE attendance SET Medical_id = NULL;
UPDATE attendance SET Status = 'Present', Medical_id = NULL;

UPDATE attendance a
INNER JOIN medical_record m 
ON a.Reg_no = m.reg_no AND a.Session_date = m.session_date
SET a.Status = 'Medical',
    a.Medical_id = m.Medical_id;


UPDATE attendance 
SET Status = 'Absent' 
WHERE Status = 'Present' 
AND Medical_id IS NULL 
AND (Attendance_id % 7 = 0)  
LIMIT 180;

-- views


DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_attend_with_medical_approval;

CREATE VIEW vw_attend_with_medical_approval AS
SELECT 
    a.attendance_id,
    a.reg_no,
    a.course_code,
    a.session_date,
    a.session_type,
    a.session_hours,
    CASE 
        WHEN a.status = 'Medical' AND m.Approved=TRUE THEN 'Present/M'
        WHEN a.status = 'Medical' AND m.Approved=FALSE THEN 'Absent/M'
        ELSE a.status
    END AS status
FROM attendance a
LEFT JOIN Medical_record m ON 
    a.Medical_id = m.Medical_id;




DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_eligibi_without_medical_with_name;

CREATE VIEW vw_eligibi_without_medical_with_name AS
SELECT
    u.L_name,
    a.reg_no,
    a.course_code,
    a.session_type,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END) AS attended_hours,
    SUM(a.session_hours) AS total_hours,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS attended_sessions,
    COUNT(*) AS total_sessions,
    ROUND(
        (SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END) / 
        SUM(a.session_hours)
    ) * 100, 2
    ) AS attendance_percentage,
    CASE 
	WHEN
	    ROUND(
            (SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'Present' THEN session_hours ELSE 0 END) / 
            SUM(session_hours)
            ) * 100, 2
    ) >=80 THEN 'Eligible' 
	   ELSE 'Not Eligible'
	END AS eligibility

FROM attendance a
LEFT JOIN student s ON
s.reg_no=a.reg_no
LEFT JOIN user u ON
u.user_id=s.user_id
GROUP BY a.reg_no, a.course_code, a.session_type
ORDER BY a.reg_no, a.course_code, a.session_type;





DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_attend_percentage_with_medical_with_eligibility_with_name;

CREATE VIEW vw_attend_percentage_with_medical_with_eligibility_with_name AS
SELECT 
 u.L_name,
    a.reg_no,
    a.course_code,
    a.session_type,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END) AS attended_hours,
    SUM(session_hours) AS total_hours,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN 1  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS attended_sessions,
    COUNT(*) AS total_sessions,
    
    ROUND( (
 SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END)/ SUM(a.session_hours)
    ) * 100, 2
    ) AS attendance_percentage,
    CASE 
	WHEN    
 SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END)/ SUM(a.session_hours)
    
     >= 0.8 AND SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'Present' THEN session_hours ELSE 0 END) / SUM(session_hours) < 0.8 THEN 'Eligible with medical' 
 WHEN    ROUND( (
 SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END)/ SUM(a.session_hours)
    ) * 100, 2
    ) >= 80 THEN 'Eligible' 
        ELSE 'Not Eligible'
    END AS eligibility
FROM attendance a
LEFT JOIN student s ON
s.reg_no=a.reg_no
LEFT JOIN user u ON
u.user_id=s.user_id
LEFT JOIN medical_record m ON
a.medical_id=m.medical_id
GROUP BY a.reg_no, a.course_code, a.session_type
ORDER BY a.reg_no, a.course_code, a.session_type;




DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_eligibi_without_medical_with_name_without_S_type;

CREATE VIEW vw_eligibi_without_medical_with_name_without_S_type AS
SELECT
    u.L_name,
    a.reg_no,
    a.course_code,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END) AS attended_hours,
    SUM(a.session_hours) AS total_hours,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS attended_sessions,
    COUNT(*) AS total_sessions,
    ROUND(
        (SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END) / 
        SUM(a.session_hours)
    ) * 100, 2
    ) AS attendance_percentage,
    CASE 
	WHEN
	    ROUND(
            (SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'Present' THEN session_hours ELSE 0 END) / 
            SUM(session_hours)
            ) * 100, 2
    ) >=80 THEN 'Eligible' 
	   ELSE 'Not Eligible'
	END AS eligibility

FROM attendance a
LEFT JOIN student s ON
s.reg_no=a.reg_no
LEFT JOIN user u ON
u.user_id=s.user_id
GROUP BY a.reg_no, a.course_code
ORDER BY a.reg_no, a.course_code;






DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_attend_percen_with_medical_with_elig_without_S_type_with_n;

CREATE VIEW vw_attend_percen_with_medical_with_elig_without_S_type_with_n AS
SELECT 
 u.L_name,
    a.reg_no,
    a.course_code,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END) AS attended_hours,
    SUM(session_hours) AS total_hours,
    SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN 1  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS attended_sessions,
    COUNT(*) AS total_sessions,
    
    ROUND( (
 SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END)/ SUM(a.session_hours)
    ) * 100, 2
    ) AS attendance_percentage,
    CASE
	WHEN    
 SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END)/ SUM(a.session_hours)
    
     >= 0.8 AND SUM(CASE WHEN status = 'Present' THEN session_hours ELSE 0 END) / SUM(session_hours) < 0.8 THEN 'Eligible with medical' 
 WHEN    ROUND( (
 SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Present' THEN a.session_hours  WHEN a.status='Medical' AND m.approved=TRUE THEN a.session_hours ELSE 0 END)/ SUM(a.session_hours)
    ) * 100, 2
    ) >= 80 THEN 'Eligible' 
        ELSE 'Not Eligible'
    END AS eligibility
FROM attendance a
LEFT JOIN student s ON
s.reg_no=a.reg_no
LEFT JOIN user u ON
u.user_id=s.user_id
LEFT JOIN medical_record m ON
a.medical_id=m.medical_id
GROUP BY a.reg_no, a.course_code
ORDER BY a.reg_no, a.course_code;



DELETE FROM mark 
WHERE Marks_type IN ('End_theory', 'End_practical')
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 
    FROM vw_attend_percen_with_medical_with_elig_without_S_type_with_n vw
    WHERE vw.eligibility = 'Not Eligible'
    AND vw.reg_no = mark.Reg_no
    AND vw.course_code = mark.Course_code
);









DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_ENG1222_marks;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_ICT1212_marks;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_ICT1222_marks;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_ICT1233_marks;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_ICT1242_marks;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_ICT1253_marks;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_TCS1212_marks;
DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_TMS1233_marks;



CREATE VIEW vw_TMS1233_marks AS
SELECT DISTINCT
    m.Reg_no,
    m.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0)
    END AS `Quiz_1(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0)
    END AS `Quiz_2(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
    END AS `End_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND((
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0) * 0.2
        ),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_Marks(40%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND((IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                  AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                  AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0) * 0.6),2)
    END AS `Total_End_Marks(60%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
        THEN 'WH'
        ELSE ROUND((
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0) * 0.2 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TMS1233'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0) * 0.6
        ),2)
    END AS `Final_Marks(100%)`
FROM 
    mark m
WHERE 
    m.Course_code = 'TMS1233';









CREATE VIEW vw_TCS1212_marks AS
SELECT DISTINCT
    m.Reg_no,
    m.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0)
    END AS `Quiz_1(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)
    END AS `Assignment_1(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
    END AS `End_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND((
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) * 0.05 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0) * 0.05 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0) * 0.2
        ),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_Marks(30%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND( ( IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0) * 0.7),2)
    END AS `Total_End_Marks(70%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
        THEN 'WH'  
        ELSE ROUND( (
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) * 0.05 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0) * 0.05 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0) * 0.2 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'TCS1212'
                      AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0) * 0.7
        ),2)
    END AS `Final_Marks(100%)`
FROM 
    mark m
WHERE 
    m.Course_code = 'TCS1212';









CREATE VIEW vw_ICT1253_marks AS
SELECT DISTINCT
    m.Reg_no,
    m.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE GREATEST(
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
        )
    END AS `Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE (
            (
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            GREATEST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
        )
    END AS `Second_Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)
    END AS `Assignment_1(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_practical(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0) 
    END AS `End_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_Practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_Practical'), 0) 
    END AS `End_practical(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND( (
            (((
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            - LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )) * 0.5) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0) * 0.2 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0) * 0.1
        ),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_Marks(40%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
             OR (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                 WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                   AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                   AND Marks_type = 'End_Practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND( (
            (IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0) * 0.4) +
            (IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_Practical'), 0) * 0.2)
        ),2) 
    END AS `Total_End_Marks(60%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Assignment_1', 'Mid_practical', 'End_theory', 'End_Practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
             OR (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                 WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253'
                   AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                   AND Marks_type = 'End_Practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'WH'
        ELSE ROUND(  (
            (((
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            - LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )) * 0.5) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0) * 0.2 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0) * 0.1 +
            (IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0) * 0.4) +
            (IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1253' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_Practical'), 0) * 0.2)
        ),2) 
    END AS `Final_Marks(100%)`
FROM 
    mark m
WHERE 
    m.Course_code = 'ICT1253';









CREATE VIEW vw_ICT1222_marks AS
SELECT DISTINCT
    m.Reg_no,
    m.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE GREATEST(
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
        )
    END AS `Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE (
            (
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            GREATEST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
        )
    END AS `Second_Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_practical(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mini_project'), 0)
    END AS `Mini_project(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical'), 0)
    END AS `End_practical(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND( (
            (((
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            - LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )) * 0.5) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mini_project'), 0) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0) * 0.2
        ),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_marks(40%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND((IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical'), 0) * 0.6),2)
    END AS `Total_End_marks(60%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = m.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222'
                AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type = 'End_practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'WH'
        ELSE ROUND( (
            (((
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            - LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )) * 0.5) * 0.1 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0) * 0.2 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical'), 0) * 0.6 +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1222' AND Reg_no = m.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mini_project'), 0) * 0.1
        ),2)
    END AS `Final_marks(100%)`
FROM 
    mark m
WHERE 
    m.Course_code = 'ICT1222';














CREATE VIEW vw_ENG1222_marks AS
SELECT 
    e.Reg_no,
    e.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Assignment_1', 'Assignment_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)
    END AS `Assignment_1(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Assignment_1', 'Assignment_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_2'), 0)
    END AS `Assignment_2(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Assignment_1', 'Assignment_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Assignment_1', 'Assignment_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                      AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
    END AS `End_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Assignment_1', 'Assignment_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND( (
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)*0.1
            +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                      AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_2'), 0)*0.1
            +
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                      AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)*0.1
        ),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_marks(30%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Assignment_1', 'Assignment_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND(( IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                    WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                      AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                      AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)*0.7),2)
    END AS `Total_End_marks(70%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Assignment_1', 'Assignment_2', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'WH'
        ELSE ROUND( (
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                        WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                          AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                          AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                        WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                          AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                          AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_2'), 0)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                        WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                          AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                          AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
                        WHERE Course_code = 'ENG1222'
                          AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no  
                          AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)*0.7
            ),2)
    END AS `Final_marks(100%)`
FROM 
    enrollment e
WHERE 
    e.Course_code = 'ENG1222';















CREATE VIEW vw_ICT1212_marks AS
SELECT 
    e.Reg_no,
    e.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE GREATEST(
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
        )
    END AS `Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE (
            (
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            GREATEST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
        )
    END AS `Second_Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
    END AS `End_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND( ((((
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                )
                - LEAST(
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                ))*0.5)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)*0.2),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_marks(30%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND(( IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)*0.7),2)
    END AS `Total_End_marks(70%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'WH'
        ELSE ROUND( (
                (((
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                )
                - LEAST(
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                ))*0.5)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)*0.2
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1212' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)*0.7
            ),2)
    END AS `Final_Marks(100%)`
FROM 
     enrollment e
WHERE 
    e.Course_code = 'ICT1212';




















CREATE VIEW vw_ICT1233_marks AS
SELECT 
    e.Reg_no,
    e.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE GREATEST(
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
        )
    END AS `Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE (
            (
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            GREATEST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
        )
    END AS `Second_Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_practical(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mini_project'), 0)
    END AS `Mini_project(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
             OR (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
    END AS `End_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
             OR (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical'), 0)
    END AS `End_practical(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND( ((((
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                )
                - LEAST(
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                ))*0.5)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mini_project'), 0)*0.2
        ),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_marks(40%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
             OR (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND((((IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
                +                 
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical'), 0))*0.5)*0.6),2)
    END AS `Total_End_marks(60%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_practical', 'Mini_project', 'End_theory', 'End_practical')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL 
             OR (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical') IS NULL 
        THEN 'WH'
        ELSE ROUND( (
                (((
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                )
                - LEAST(
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                ))*0.5)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_practical'), 0)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mini_project'), 0)*0.2
                +
                ((IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
                 +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1233' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_practical'), 0))*0.5)*0.6
            ),2)
    END AS `Final_Marks(100%)`
FROM 
     enrollment e
WHERE 
    e.Course_code = 'ICT1233';

















CREATE VIEW vw_ICT1242_marks AS
SELECT 
    e.Reg_no,
    e.Course_code,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE GREATEST(
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
            IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
        )
    END AS `Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE (
            (
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            GREATEST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
            -
            LEAST(
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
            )
        )
    END AS `Second_Max_Quiz(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)
    END AS `Mid_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)
    END AS `Assignment_1(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)
    END AS `End_theory(100%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        ELSE ROUND(((((
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                )
                - LEAST(
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                ))*0.5)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)*0.15
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)*0.05
        ),2)
    END AS `Total_CA_Marks(30%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'E*(Not_eligible)'
        ELSE ROUND((IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)*0.7),2)
    END AS `Total_END_Marks(70%)`,
    CASE 
        WHEN (SELECT Student_type FROM student WHERE Reg_no = e.Reg_no) = 'Suspend' THEN 'WH'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark 
              WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242'
                AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no 
                AND Marks_type IN ('Quiz_1', 'Quiz_2', 'Quiz_3', 'Mid_theory', 'Assignment_1', 'End_theory')
                AND Marks_value = 'MC') IS NOT NULL THEN 'MC'
        WHEN (SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory') IS NULL THEN 'WH'
        ELSE ROUND( ((((
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0) +
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                )
                - LEAST(
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_1'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_2'), 0),
                    IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Quiz_3'), 0)
                ))*0.5)*0.1
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Mid_theory'), 0)*0.15
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'Assignment_1'), 0)*0.05
                +
                IFNULL((SELECT Marks_value FROM mark WHERE Course_code = 'ICT1242' AND Reg_no = e.Reg_no AND Marks_type = 'End_theory'), 0)*0.7
        ),2)
    END AS `Final_Marks(100%)`
FROM 
 enrollment e
WHERE 
    e.Course_code = 'ICT1242';






DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_CA_Status;
CREATE VIEW vw_CA_Status AS
SELECT 
    Reg_no,
    Course_code,
    CA_marks,
    CASE 
        WHEN Course_code IN ('ICT1222', 'ICT1233', 'ICT1253', 'TMS1233') THEN 
            CASE WHEN CA_marks >= 16 THEN 'CA_Pass' ELSE 'CA_Fail' END
        WHEN Course_code IN ('ENG1222', 'ICT1212', 'ICT1242', 'TCS1212') THEN 
            CASE WHEN CA_marks >= 12 THEN 'CA_Pass' ELSE 'CA_Fail' END
        ELSE 'Unknown'
    END AS CA_Status
FROM (

    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(30%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_ENG1222_marks
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(30%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_ICT1212_marks
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(40%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_ICT1222_marks
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(40%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_ICT1233_marks
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(30%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_ICT1242_marks
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(40%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_ICT1253_marks
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(30%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_TCS1212_marks
    
    UNION ALL
    
    SELECT 
        Reg_no,
        Course_code,
        `Total_CA_marks(40%)` AS CA_marks
    FROM vw_TMS1233_marks
) AS all_ca_marks
ORDER BY reg_no, course_code;



DROP VIEW IF EXISTS vw_end_exam_elig_basedON_CA_and_Atten;

CREATE VIEW vw_end_exam_elig_basedON_CA_and_Atten AS
SELECT 
    cp.Reg_no,
    cp.Course_code,
    cp.CA_Status,
    COALESCE(att.eligibility, 'Not Eligible') AS Attendance_Eligibility,
    CASE 
        WHEN cp.CA_Status = 'CA_Pass' AND att.eligibility IN ('Eligible', 'Eligible with medical') THEN 'Eligible'
        ELSE 'Not Eligible'
